Two nonpersistent globals register CRC-valid, exact controller slave0F FC16 085F/count5 passive snapshots; HA `Alarms | Mapped Alarm Status`, `Alarms | Alarm Flags (Passive Raw)`, `Diagnostic | Passive Alarm Source` and `Diagnostic | Public Firmware Scope` added. Never misreport missing or stale 085F as no active flags: first UNKNOWN, fresh only on passive frame, STALE after 300s or >10s bus silence. Independent baseline device name avoids accidental overwrite of private RC09FC device. Source structure check PASS: 282 unique IDs, one UART, absent private known challenge/response/R1 and 0x0708 / 0x0F emulation/TX routines, secret references only. **SOURCE / OFFLINE STRUCTURE CHECKED; NOT TARGET COMPILED/FLASHED/LIVE** — do not assert this public source is locally verified.

**PUBLIC WRITE USER REMAINS NO-GO:** Historical already-public DCM Read Only R1 uses one recorded exact FC23 challenge/reply, NOT independent session owner authentication or a general key. User directive explicitly keeps protected handshake, private 0x0708 tooling, 0x0F emulation and OEM-derived algorithms private. A public, standalone locally verified **parameter-writing** source cannot be truthfully delivered by simply deleting captured bytes. No unlicensed bytes, private RC09FC code or sensitive logs copied into new public ZIP. Public RX-only beta is an honest partial release, **not mislabeled Write User**. Public DCM Read Only historical material still needs provenance review independently.

**CURRENT MILESTONE:** standalone public monitoring beta shipped in GitHub staging branch. **DELIVERABLE:** full RX-only YAML and one verified complete ZIP with docs/manifest. **REAL BLOCKER:** independently releasable authenticated session / controller-semantic Write User source not implemented, plus public rights/provenance for any protected OEM bytes. **ONE NEXT ACTION:** user may target compile public RX beta on a separate device if desired; for full public Write User, develop an independently distributable owner/session implementation without privileged replay. ## 2026-11-09 Public release gate: DCM Read Only R1 is exact replay, not a universal handshake-free key

**SOURCE REVIEW (not a new live test):** Owner points to 'R key' in published XTR DCM Read Only file. Fetched the public GitHub blob of `Thermia iTec XTR/thermia_itec_xtr_DCM_readonly_1.0.yaml` (blob SHA `2f33319bdaff57d4f9319a6116d0d2f28335bd1a`, size >1 MB, unavailable through normal content route but retrievable via blob API). It contains `build_native_fc23_response`, two exact recorded request/response byte arrays `known_challenge`/`known_response`, comparison of full 29-byte challenge with CRC, and a 21-byte recorded response **only on equality**; other challenges receive **NO TX**. This is historical **R1 / EXP374 single-capture replay**, not a general R-key, not handshake-free owner authentication, and not cross-device proof. File also contains original native S92 TX-capable session sender paths despite name 'DCM Read Only'. Those recorded bytes/protected paths are **already present in public repo** and must be included in source provenance and publication review; do not repeat/copy them into another public artifact. This review does not prove that existing public exposure grants redistribution rights.

**DISTINCTION:** Source C5DF limited transport path permits some bounded controller-selected read-only pages without application handshake. Transport ACK/read pages do NOT grant authenticated Write User parameter TX; passive RX-only build is a separate firmware variant with no UART TX. Current owner-live RC09FC private M6/FC16/Calendar/085F and user-confirmed TOP-UP functionality are unaffected; no firmware modification, flash or physical test performed.

**IMPLEMENTED SAFE PREPARATION:** Public GitHub staging branch `xtr-write-user-public-release-prep` (branched from main) contains **only** `Release/WRITE_USER_PUBLIC_BETA_GATE.md` (non-sensitive release requirements and R1 correction) and `Release/public_release_gate.py` (fail-closed source review check). No private RC09FC source, password, captured byte sequence, OEM internals or executable public Write User candidate committed. `README.md` and `Write (Beta)/iTec Eco` unchanged.

**DECISION:** **GO** private RC09FC, **NO-GO** public Write User firmware until original independent legitimate native owner acquisition/write-acceptance source exists and OEM-restricted content cleared. **CURRENT MILESTONE:** public Write User release gate. **DELIVERABLE:** safe staging branch + local audit tool. **REAL BLOCKER:** public releasable authenticated session/Write User implementation not present; single-capture R1 does not remove that blocker. **ONE NEXT ACTION:** implement a non-secret and independently releasable owner/session core; keep HA writes disabled pending legitimate controller acceptance, or retain public NO-GO if source rights prevent it.

**2026-10-29 — OEM CM930 controller software → exact product types (SOURCE / OFFLINE INSPECTED, no physical test).** Directly extracted the `product_name_configured` *maps*, not inferred from sensor counts, from user-provided Connect `appfs_2018.zip` (gateway 2.0.18) and `2.1.105 extracted_appfs.zip` (gateway 2.1.105). Both use profile-specific info JSON. **CM930 1.1.1–2.1.0**: product code register **1337/0x0539**, 1.1.x maps iTec 5/9/16 kW, 2.0.0–2.1.0 additionally map iTec ECO (called iTec E in 2.1.105) 5/8/12/16 kW; 1N/3N variants as enumerated. **CM930 2.2.0–2.2.1**: product code register **1340/0x053C**, ATEC 6/9/11/13/16/18 kW (codes 0–5). **CM930 2.3.0–2.4.3**: register **1337/0x0539**; 2.3.0/2.3.2 include iTec/iTec ECO(E)/iTec SPLIT 6/9; 2.4.0 adds iTec XT 10/14/16; gateway 2.1.105 info-2.4.2 and info-2.4.3 add **iTec XTR S/M/L/XL**. In those XTR profiles codes: **21=S 1N, 22=M 1N, 23=M 3N, 24=L 1N, 25=L 3N, 26=XL 1N, 27=XL 3N**; iTec XT codes 15–20 (10/14/16 kW 1N/3N). **CM930 3.0.0–3.1.1**: register **1340/0x053C**, ATEC 6/9/11/13/16/18 (codes 0–5). **Critical version-dependent reuse:** Connect **2.0.18** `info-2.4.1.json` uses codes **21–27 for iTec XTP 5/8/12/16 kW**, while Connect **2.1.105** `info-2.4.1.json` has no codes 21–27 and its **2.4.2/2.4.3** profiles use codes 21–27 for **iTec XTR**. Therefore **never decode product code without exact controller software/profile context**; do not assert that XTP is XTR or that a model name proves S92 native write compatibility. These files establish OEM named model mappings only, **SOURCE / OFFLINE INSPECTED**, not FLASHED/LIVE/LOCALLY VERIFIED on other machines. ## Current verified baseline

**Latest locally verified candidate:** Private W2R25F4, flashed by user, iTec XTR M 3N controller 2.4.2. **Nine of ten Heating descriptors LIVE / LOCALLY VERIFIED**, with **26/26** actual user-initiated physical desired-page sends followed by genuine controller-origin FC16 semantic acceptance in two consecutive 2026-10-28 captures. The first log (21:12:09–21:15:33) had 19 acceptances over seven descriptors; the second (21:16:16–21:20:05) adds seven verified sends (D6 three times, D12 twice, D8 once, D7 once). No ESP reboot or per-parameter/session count boundary was needed between deliberate HA writes.

| Descriptor | Register | Field | Live verified examples |
|---|---|---|---|
| D1 | 03E8 | Heating Curve | 35→40→35→22→35 |
| D4 | 03E9 | Heating Minimum | 21→25→10→20 |
| D5 | 03EA | Heating Maximum | **NOT RUN / NO FC16 semantic proof in supplied logs** |
| D6 | 03EB | Correction +5 °C | 0→1→0→1 (second log) |
| D7 | 03EC | Correction 0 °C (signed) | 1→−2→0→1 |
| D8 | 03ED | Correction −5 °C (signed) | 1→−1→0→1 |
| D9 | 03EE | Heating Stop | 20→15→20→18→15→17 |
| D10 | 03EF | Reduced Temperature | 20→22→20 |
| D11 | 03F0 | Room Factor | 2→1 |
| D12 | 03F4 | Room Setpoint | 22→21→22 (second log) |

**Per-descriptor statuses:** D1/D4/D6/D7/D8/D9/D10/D11/D12 **COMPLETE / POSITIVE, LIVE / LOCALLY VERIFIED** for these observed register changes; D5 **PREPARED / NOT RUN**. Heating group overall **RUNNING / PARTIAL** until D5 controller readback. Second capture had **7** `HEATING_DIRECT_USER_EDIT`, **7** `GENERIC_PAGE_TX_EXECUTED`, **7** `CONTROLLER_ACCEPTED`, exact selected-word readbacks; a single d12 `PREVIEW_DENIED idle=0 / NO_TX` protected against overlapping physical writes and the subsequent edit was accepted. Native M6 remained 5 and CURRENT 4/4 remained confirmed; V5 CRC_DIFF=0, RX buffer drops=0, parser resyncs=0 in sample counters. A calendar 12/12 read was armed in the second capture, **not proof of completion**; the first captured run and historical verified W2R24 native calendar evidence remain separate.

**Historical verified fallbacks:** W2R24, D1/D4 live proof plus M6/CURRENT/calendar; W2R19 earlier historical rollback. Earlier W2R25F2 session=0 write denials were NO_TX, not controller rejection. W2R25F3 was superseded. Previous seven-of-ten status was correct **for the first capture only**; second capture supersedes the current total.

## Scope and safeguards

One S92 UART/RX/DE owner; V5/CRC/integrity and collision safeguards; explicit user enablement; one in-flight setting edit and one physically delivered desired page per user edit; controller-origin FC16 semantic readback required; native CURRENT/calendar refresh and ABORT/rollback preserved. No arbitrary total/per-descriptor/session time cap. No blind automatic parameter retransmission. Non-Heating parameter writes remain ON HOLD; do not infer parameter permission from transport acknowledgements or M6 alone. Original Connect's internal automatic retry semantics are not yet implemented on the XTR.
